- **Music player**: lazy-mounts the YouTube iframe on first play intent (idle sidebar: no `.yt-music-frame`, no iframe_api script); track swaps via postMessage `loadVideoById` — iframe src NEVER changes; adjudicate track by `cpn=`/`docid=` in `/api/stats/watchtime` or change-data `music.mood`; third-party doubleclick CORS console errors after playback are embed noise — attribute by trailing `@ URL`.
- **Rest/latch unimplemented**: `until_next_turn_extendable` ZERO consumers; STR-check advantage never produced (isSave-only modifiers); heavy-armor DONNING gate absent except Mage Armor step-16 strip + Falcon (`isWearingArmor`); full-concentration-duration→Petrified has NO producer (petrified absent from expirations/concentration clears) — ladder-only via 3 GM-record clicks; shake-awake registered (index.js:532) but NO UI producer; "Cast as Ritual" toggle absent — slot ALWAYS consumed, zero-slot popup DISABLES cast (RAW slotless impossible, spellPreparationService lacks spellbook-ritual check); Resourceful spend-as-advantage ZERO consumers (checkbox is the only surface); spellThief lv-eligibility gate grep-zero; possession leg advisory.
- **Summon/spirit gaps**: exact-name join-match gap — suffixed variants ("Bestial Spirit (Land)") join FULL HP despite halves stamps (initiativeService.js:114); summon AC ladder flag on spells.json is no-op (monsters.json only); Find Steed HP scales by SLOT not caster level (accepted); summon-cap counters/corpse-age clock unmodeled.
- **Reaction/affordance gaps**: Reactive Strike reach-entry was unwired (now gated: latch→polearm-equipped→armed target→isWithinRange, `{type:'attack_roll'}` popup, `reactive_strike_refused`); Pole Strike gate fires next round without Attack action (no round/attacker latch, always-rendered row, click-time-only weapon-name popup check); Charger chooser re-offers EVERY melee hit un-gated, Cancel-safe; Hew (bonus_action_attack on crit/kill) route exists automation/index.js but never fires live — dead lane; Sentinel Guardian router/handler dead (feats.json uses generic reaction_damage), stale re-press self-targets, Escape to abandon; no 1-min expiry clock on many self-buffs (Innate Sorcery until-LR, Psychic Veil 1h, Rogue Evasion, Superior Defense cleared by initiative-roll wipe instead); save-forcing break on invisibility-type buffs has no consumer (applyDamage-only); `_<buff>_refused` logs missing on some refusal lanes (popup-only).
- **Data-shape dead lanes**: `uses:"proficiency_bonus"` STRING never registers in `_trackedResources` (tracker max falls back 1, refusal-at-0 still fires); `distanceExpression:"speed/2"` zero consumers; `hp_threshold`/advisory families per durable-extraction; `if damage ... else effect` dispatch strands riders on damage options; unarmed lastAttack collapses to `weaponType:'melee'/isUnarmedStrike:false` at handlePlainDamage (~:356) — author-riders gated on weaponType==='unarmed' never match (gate isUnarmedStrike upstream); Swift Quiver BA row builders must set BOTH `type` and `actionType` (CharBonusActions.jsx:867 filters `attack.type`); live equipment `range` is NUMERIC `{normal,long}` not '80_ft' strings; `modal load-effects` must never depend on inline-prop defaults (`excludeTypes=[]` literals loop → tab crash); trait→cantrip-row automations need the `buildCantripAbilityContainer`/`ensureSpellAbilitiesContainer` slotless-container support (spellCalc2024.js:569/:309) — unsupported passive types return null before grants; production classes.json carries class-level `spell_casting_ability` (Arcane Trickster INT) — container-stamp overwrites must be marker-guarded; 2024 disk chars store `{name,subclass}` only — code indexing `class.class_levels[N]` silently mis-falls-back (grep class_levels disk-index sites when bonus math is off); modal spell-lane confirm lane re-casts on repeated sp-roll-btn clicks; raw char JSON abilities lack computed `bonus` (synthesize `Math.floor((base+inc)/2)-5` for module probes).
- **Ritual/race model notes**: Dwarf Stonecunning 2024 app model = BA Tremorsense 60ft/10min uses=PB LR-recharge (NOT PHB History bonus); fresh RACE-SWAP bulk-seeds trait counters at 0 → run Long Rest before activation probes post-swap; "Short or Long Rest" feature specs need explicit short-rest reset entry.
- **Sticky-latch gaps**: `dreadAmbushSpeedActive`-style one-writer latches with zero combat-reset seams (fixed pattern: reset in handleClear + buildInitiativeUpdates + clearPlayerRoundFlags); per-cast distributor latches only via monotonic token stamps; Riposte LOSES `_Riposte_usedRound` stamp when the riposte MISS-resolves (same-round ungated re-fire+spend — GET ~11s post to confirm); OA transport lives ONLY in usePopupAttack (standalone sheet OA rows must forward `{isOpportunityAttack:true, opportunityAttackerName}`).
- **Extra PC-lane seams (fill-in)**: GWF truth fields on damage entries = `gwfOriginalRolls/gwfDisplayRolls/gwfApplied` (live lane rules/core/greatWeaponFighting.js + automationPassives + useLoggedDiceRollDamage — manifest handler paths stale). Dueling gate = meleeCount===1 && rangedCount===0 (attackCalc2024.js:183) — two-handed NOT excluded (count-proxy leak, honest gap). Death-save advantage seam = DeathSavingThrows hasSaveModifier('death_save_saving_throws')→rollDeathSaveWithAdvantage; feat grants collect via SAVE_MODIFIER_BUILDERS.conditional_advantage; SSE damage-prompt path ALWAYS single-rolls (advantage is sheet-seam only); non-holder ledger = rolls:null single d20, holder = rolls:[a,b] higher kept. Skill checks: sheet `span.clickable "<Skill> (+N)"` auto-rolls at CLICK (log stamps immediately; Done only dismisses); roll popup second value is mode-normal spare; total=rolls[0]+bonus. Save cell = 4th child of `.abilities` row (name|score|mod|save); ability row clickables order [name,check,save]. Portent skill face writes TOP-LEVEL lastAttack (not cd.campaign.lastAttack); can replace a monster's lastAttack (`portentUsed/portentOriginalD20`). Quivering Palm: requires Open Hand major; row click SETS (FP-4, quivering_palm:'<T>' te), later row rolls CON DC 8+WIS+PB 10d12 full/half; "Release the Harmless Vibrations" = zero-cost clear; single-target gate reopens existing modal zero-spend; every unarmed hit pops the Empowered Strikes Force chooser (Skip). Sneak Attack auto-fire gate order: rogue→dice→finesse/ranged→disadvantage=0→once-per-turn latch→advantage→flanking (gridless lenient-true); latch reset = round-walk, NOT admin-clear on a loaded tab (full-store resync resurrects); foreign stale te (death_strike) re-surfaces save prompts every sheet mount — audit targetEffects before rows; killing the last hostile self-terminates cs — bump HP first. Soul Knife PE pool key ABSENT = fallback full. Resistance cantrip: te `resistance_damage_reduction`+chosenType, reduction truth = ability_use "reduced damage by N (1d4)" + hp_change==final; once/turn latch clears EVERY turn boundary (looser than RAW); mismatch shows `resistanceRoll:null`.
- **Turn Undead / searing lane**: CD row → SetConditionModal (NPC saves inline auto-roll) → `turn-undead-result{failedTargets}` → `${wisMod}d8` shared pool rolled ONCE per use, total applied to each failed undead (Radiant, sequential awaits); non-undead auto-excluded; force-fail via cs POST wis:-2/hp:999, force-save wis:+19. Searing damage popups persist and block the next CD press — flush `.popup-close-btn` between uses.
- **Aura tick gap**: aura damage tick damages ANY non-holder in range when no ally list is stored (no enemy filter) — Next-cycles hit PCs first.
- **Map autosave (shipped behavior)**: useMapLoader second useEffect = debounced 1s (`AUTOSAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS`) FULL-snapshot PUT `/api/campaigns/:c/maps/<kebab-mapname>` (last-write-wins-safe), pending in ref + `flushSave` on pointerup/leave/unmount/campaign-switch (pending carries own campaign/mapName); initial empty-map creation save stays direct. Paint/Erase toolbar buttons TOGGLE OFF on re-click — check aria-pressed before strokes or the stroke silently becomes a PAN (zero writes, zero errors). Live shape: 0 PUTs mid-stroke, 1 at pointerup.
- **Post-reload carry-over recipe**: pre-reload handler setItem sessionStorage flag; App-boot consumes it via autoSelectCampaign prop (ref-guarded), target view set at the TAIL of the select callback (last setActiveView wins). Hook-order traps: TDZ — a hook callback listed in an EARLIER useEffect dep array throws "Cannot access before initialization"; declare the hook call above the effect.

## 10. Exploration additions 2026-10-10
- Character wizard create PUTs `/api/campaigns/:c/<Name>.json` as blind upsert — a duplicate name DESTROYS the existing character file. ALWAYS `curl GET <Name>.json` to backup BEFORE any wizard test; restore = `cp backup file.json` THEN `curl -X PUT` the same JSON (loaded tabs + in-memory cache resurrect via full-store POSTs otherwise); verify disk 12s post-PUT before trusting restore.
- Wizard step refs churn constantly (DOM re-renders on every field write) — re-`find` Next/Save after every keystroke group; final-step footer button is "Create Character" (indicator "✓ Save" stays disabled forever, cosmetic).
- Empty rename commits are silently ignored (maps revert name in-row); dup rename shows inline banner; renames rename the map FILE slug within ~12s.
- Faction form is FLAT (name/desc/goals/notes/influence slider min=1 max=10 — "0" fill throws Malformed value, DOM min enforcement); dup-name error appears only after Save-click and persists after fixing name; delete uses window.confirm.
- NPCs management list does NOT live-sync across GM tabs (mount-fetch only): created NPCs never arrive, deleted NPCs never leave the other tab until re-nav. Open edit forms stay stale (no squash) — concurrent saves = silent lost-update (last-write-wins, no version stamp). Judge truth via `GET /api/campaigns/:c/npcs`.
- Sidebar dice tray = pure client-local popup; ZERO writes to `/log` and no SSE (DiceTray.jsx grep-zero for log/addEntry). Log view empty-state text "Roll dice … to get started" is a broken promise (bug-dice-tray-rolls-not-logged).
- Music panel: mood-chip click AUTO-PLAYS embedded YouTube iframe (Play flips to Pause unbidden); audio depends on network YouTube — expect failures in offline/sandboxed runs, don't chase as app defect; Stop halts.
- `.dice-tray-popup-overlay` survives tab switches and intercepts all page clicks — press Escape before clicking page elements after any dice roll.
- Admin Clear Change Data / Clear Campaign Log fire native confirms; post-clear verified `change-data {}` + `log []` even while two tabs open (quiet-state worked this session; still clear last).

## 12. Character wizard / character-file routes (added by wizard-dup-name fix)
- Character create is `POST /api/campaigns/:campaign` (filename derived from name) — PUT `/:Name.json` is edit-only; overwrite bugs on create live in the POST handler (`campaigns-character.js` writeFileSync upsert).
- `campaigns-character.test.js` mocks `fs.existsSync` blanket-true; any file-existence guard must switch those mocks to path-aware impls (`p => !p.endsWith('.json')`).
- `vi.mock('fs')` factory may not reference a top-level const (hoisting) — inline the functions like `npcs-duplicate-name.test.js`.
- Adding exports to `src/config/utils.js` breaks `CharacterCreationWizard.submission.test.jsx` partial mock — extend `vi.mock` + exact-args assertions same pass.
- Residual (ticket owed): edit-mode rename onto an existing character name still overwrites via PUT — needs maps-rename-guard twin.
- Wizard UI content headings ("Step 6: Skill Proficiencies") differ from internal `steps-config` numbers (skills = step 10) — `stepValidators` must key on the internal number. Skill-cap enforcement: `validateSkillsStep` (config/utils.js stepValidators[10]) + checkbox refusal guard in WizardStepSkills via `getProficiencyCapBlockReason`; pre-selected skills and null limits must stay ungated.
- `.dice-tray-popup-overlay` dismisses ONLY via Escape or its × — `el.click()` on the overlay does NOT close it (unlike `.mc-overlay`/`.popup-overlay` flush pattern); it intercepts Admin clicks until Escape.
- Client→log seam for GM tools: `addEntry(campaignName, entry)` (logService.js, same path as Log add-note) — server stamps id/timestamp and broadcasts via shared SSE; GM dice-tray rolls now log `{type:'roll', rollType:'dice', characterName:'GM', name:'Dice Tray <die>', rolls, total, formula}` from Sidebar.jsx.
- Wizard step numbering is now driven by ONE shared index: `getVisibleSteps({isEditing, ruleset})` (steps-config.js) stamps consecutive `displayNumber` over visible steps; WizardSidebar renders displayNumber, wizard passes `stepNumber` to every step component, and `useWizardNavigation` walks via the same visible-steps order (2026-10-10 wizard-step-numbering). Wizard footer has NO Cancel on the final step — dismiss via header ×. Walking can auto-open the Magic Initiate `.mi-overlay` when a select lands on its first option — dismiss via its "Skip for now" button.
- Wizard fast-walk recipe: whole create flow in one `run_code_unsafe` call (selectOption for dropdowns, loop Next gated on `disabled`); class step requires a subclass before Next enables. `browser_take_screenshot` filename resolves relative to workspace root and drops PNGs into the repo — save under /tmp or the .playwright-mcp root. Point-buy overspend refusal: `handleBaseScoreChange` in WizardStepAbilities.jsx (holds value, inline `.points-feedback` beside `.points-remaining`, 3s auto-clear, mirrors WizardStepSkills expertise-feedback pattern).
- NPCs list live-sync (2026-10-10): server publishes `npcs-list-<campaign>` `{action:'created'|'updated'|'deleted'|'replaced'}` from routes/npcs.js PUT + jsonEntityCrud `broadcast` option (POST/DELETE); NPCs.jsx re-fetches via Subscriber, skipped while edit modal open (draft-safe, sync resumes on close). NPC form gotcha: click is `textContent.trim()==='Save'` exact — "Save & Add to Initiative" sits beside it and an index-based click can hit it, auto-adding the NPC to the initiative board and navigating to Initiative. Initiative `npc-remove-btn` has title="Remove NPC" only (no text/aria). EventSource never appears in Chromium `performance.getEntriesByType('resource')` — count /subscribe connections via server log `[SSE] Client connected ... total:` instead (expect total == open tabs).

- New-consumer trichotomy (§388): save-gated max-reduce rider on compound row was CODE-GAP (no consumer branch, not §70 advisory — RAW mechanical, §233 parser armed, consumers proven, applyFailedSaveClauseGrants branch absent) = sanctioned consumer-code fix (§511 MA-1847 precedent, mirror byte-shape), NOT pure-data.

## MA-1878 additions (Demogorgon Gaze FIXED 2026-10-11 — variants[] chooser + te transport)
- **Te-variant chooser consumer seam**: `parseSaveVariantChooser` extended to accept variants declaring `te` OR `conditions[]` (§58 MA-1436, §384 filter guard malformed-still-filters pre-existing byte-pins). `applyFailedSaveClauseGrants` NEW fail-only consumer `grantSaveVariantClause` + `registerSaveVariantTeGrant`: consume variant.te→registerTargetEffect (MA-1847/§595 zoneTe byte-shape, ROOT te source=attacker duration until_start_of_next_turn) + ONE merged §80 clock (rounds from variant payload: "until start of next turn"→rounds:2, folded MA-1872 te-removal twin) covering conditions AND te removal. Transport: `saveVariant` stamp threaded buildAbilitySaveRollContext→fire()→inline lane (§1436 chooser lane, MA-1863 §511).
- **Prose-shape-scrape fake-Line guard census pitfall**: breathAoeShape prose-LOS-scrape /\bline\b/i matches "line of sight" description prose + numeric range → fabricated Line-60 picker (feet-token regex requires full word, "ft" misses). Fix double-gate on `action.variants && rangeToFeet(range)` byte-exact outside chooser rows — census lair_actions too (Young Green Dragon Wall of Thorns MA-1785-pinned Line picker broke single-gate version). Range "120 feet" authored kills fake-60 via real feet-token.
- **Choose-or-roll-d6 chooser LIVE (§58 MA-1436)**: GM-chosen variant rides the picker/inline chooser (press model), random-d6 auto-roll stays MA-0575 ticket-owed (no transport consumer). Confusion te registered §23 (§410 EB-NPC TURN_START_HANDLERS !playerStats-guard dead, GM-enforced residual logged, never silently claimed).
- **No-Playwright-runtime honest fallback (§90)**: browser tools absent → verified via live API-served bytes (`range/variants served`) + live module-source bundle inspection (fake-Line prose scrape dies, parser change live) + full behavioral test locks (fail-only grants §511, byte-inert without variants[] §384, §58 siblings green) + admin-curl clears GET-confirmed. Honest residuals named (§410 turn-start GM-enforced), no fabricated click claims.

## MA-1879 additions (Demogorgon Spellcasting FIXED 2026-10-11 — markup family)
- **MA-1850/MA-0421 Spellcasting markup twin**: plain-text description → `extractSpellNamesFromSpellcasting` returns [] → `SpellCastLinks` null (§258 chip gate by design). Fix = pure DATA, MA-1848/MA-1850 byte-shape inline `<strong>Tier:</strong> <em>Spell</em>` wrap (prose sentence verbatim, punctuation byte-preserved, no `<br>`), 7 resolvable spell names verified spells.json (no §599 fake-chip). Usage tokens `<strong>N/Day:</strong>` INSIDE header arms `extractSpellcastingSpellUses` → `monsterSpellUses` §58 gate live (3/Day cast-ledger refuses 4th, `(N left)` chip counter + `mc-dice-link-spell-spent` class). DC-save affordance rides cast lane (row DC chip structurally N/A Spellcasting rows MA-1294/§251).

Restore `git show HEAD:path > path` + anchored fragment `json.dumps(indent=2)` insertion on unique sibling `cleanup` key line → +8 hunk surgical (parse-check, native `\uXXXX` style native preserved). `browser_click` arg=`target` not `ref`; `browser_evaluate` results string-wrapped — JSON.parse tail before `.some()`/`.map()`.

## MA-1881 (Legendary delegation inherits a variants[] chooser — pitfalls/recipes)
- **A `delegates_to` child with empty content fields collapses to the master's FIRST clause** in `resolveLegendaryRow` (MonsterCardModal.jsx) — if the master carries a MA-1878 `variants[]` chooser, the chooser is silently dropped on the legendary surface (no chip arms, no d6, first arm always applied). Fix pattern: exported pure helper `legendaryDelegateChooserAction` copies master variants+dc+type+range onto the child BEFORE the first-clause shortcut (same MA-1853 inherit-before-collapse precedent as appendMonsterDelegation). Code-side inherit beats data mirroring — child can never drift from parent.
- **Legendary-panel modal row locators collide with the master row** (both named "Gaze"): scope by the action-cost badge (i.faa-bolt ⚡ N = legendary; 2 targets pre-spend, 1 post) to pick the LA-scoped row. Post-spend the click target migrates to the single bolt.
- Zero-save-before-pick on the legendary surface: assert `rollSavingThrow` untouched AND `data-save-action` count 0 until a chip arm is clicked; the exhausted-row (2/2) refusal is a per-frame exact-match locator.

## MA-1890 (Fenrir Devour two-field DATA gate patch — pitfalls/recipes)
- **Prose "creature X has Grappled" = `target_prerequisite:{conditions:["grappled"],by_attacker:true}`** (MA-0019 shape; by_attacker demands activeConditionMeta[cond].source===monster display name — a third-party grapple still refuses). Rows authors must NOT rely on the gate for reachability when the monster has no grapple attack (Fenrir Bite lacks hit_conditions, MA-1888) — in-app the gated row is GM-grant-only; that's honest, not a defect.
- **"(Recharge 5-6)" name-text never gates** — `rechargeUsageOf` reads only the structured `recharge` field (flat "5-6" or usage-object). Fix is one field; consumer then spends at fire, refuses with `<slug>_refused (not recharged)`, and rolls turn-start recovery d6 vs threshold 5.
- Pure-function gate spec pattern: inject `getRuntimeValue` closure over (conds, meta) — no mocks needed for evaluateTargetPrerequisiteGate; monsterRechargeGate takes the raw stored map `{Devour:{recharged:false}}`.

## MA-1898/MA-1901 (Garmr save-row DATA fixes — pitfalls/recipes)
- **Save rows only word-scan `save_effect`** (§346); a save row authoring `hit_conditions:[...]` is bait — buildHitConditionClause runs attack-lane only (§153/handlePlainDamage). Pin inertness in the spec (clause built but never consumed on save rows) so a future save-lane mis-wire can't double-grant.
- Recharge ungated fingerprint: name-text "(Recharge 5-6)" parsed by NO parser — one-field `recharge:"5-6"` fix rides MA-0031 spend/refusal/recovery lane; verify via rechargeUsageOf/monsterRechargeGate pure functions (no runtime store needed).
- HeroesFeastBard Leading Evasion chooser auto-opens before every PC save in AoE runs — Skip (decline) per target mandatory before the prompt; budget one extra click per target.

## MA-1916 (Final queue note — recharge breath-twin family complete)
- Breath-twin one-field fix now landed for MA-1843/1859/1898/1907/1916: name-text "(Recharge 5-6)" never gates; always author `recharge:"5-6"` in data; consumer rechargeUsageOf/monsterRechargeGate is pure — fingerprint spec needs no mocks.

## 2026-10-13 exploration run — NEW durable entries (non-MA lanes)
- MAP MANAGER OPEN IS THE DEAF ROUTE (filed bug-map-editor-sse-key-mismatch-json-suffix): manager "Open" passes `map.fileName` WITH `.json` into `<Map mapName>` → useSSESync expectedKey `map-data-<c>-<name>.json` never matches suffix-free publishes (maps.js:301) → GM editor tabs drop ALL live map updates (walls/players/revealed/placedItems). Never conclude "SSE broken server-side" from this route — players' auto-open (`loadActiveMapAndOpen`, App.jsx:491, strips .json) DOES sync. Debug recipe: `import('/src/services/ui/sseClient.js')` in page context → `__getSources().get(campaign)` → inspect `readyState`/`handlers.size`, wrap handlers to log `event.key` + `data.walls.length`; confirm the mounted props via fiber walk (`__reactFiber$…` → memoizedProps.mapName).
- FOG MODEL (source-verified): map JSON `{walls[], revealed[], fog[](legacy empty), gridSize, placedItems[], players[]…}`; fog = NOT-in-revealed AND NOT-in-current-LoS (useFogOfWar); LoS computed from TOKENS (`players`) + walls — with zero walls every cell stays visible so Reset Fog alone shows NO fog (revealed just compacts, e.g. stale 31200→grid-full 400). To manufacture fog you must paint walls AND have no token LoS around them; tokens sitting ON painted cells keep them revealed. Cell px: content spans `rect.grid-bg` client square, NOT the full svg element box (viewBox 800 stretched preserveAspectRatio-none) — measure grid-bg ÷ cols.
- PLAYWRIGHT: mouse events do NOT reach background tabs (drags/clicks silently lost even with fresh rects) — `bringToFront()` the acting tab; EB qty `+` and stubborn checkboxes: use `locator.click()`/`focus()+Space`, mouse-center may absorb repeatedly (extends §155); Playwright auto-accepts window.confirm/prompt instantly — to prove a dialog exists, override `window.confirm=(m)=>{record;return false}` first (handle_dialog then honestly errors "no modal state" after auto-accept). run_code_unsafe must be `async (page)=>{…}` whole-function string and `page.evaluate(fetch)` needs ABSOLUTE URLs.
- DELETE-GUARD MAP (2026-10-13): Quests=confirm-then-modal-stays-open(BUG); Sessions=modal Delete closes clean; Notes=confirm "Delete this note?" from OPEN view `.ct-btn-danger`; EB saved-encounter trash + party-inventory item delete = NO confirm at all.
  - UPDATE 2026-10-24 (FIXED): Quests modal Delete now closes on success (`handleCloseModal()` after DELETE resolves, NPCs/Notes/Factions/Sessions pattern, + setError on failure). Quests rows are `li[role=button]` — match `li[aria-label^="Edit quest:"]`, CSS `button[aria-label=…]` hits nothing; an open overlay intercepts all row clicks for 30s — Cancel first. `run_code_unsafe` has no `window`/`location` in node scope — confirm-overrides must live inside `page.evaluate`; side-effects before a ReferenceError still land.
- SESSIONS RIG: name `#session-name`; checklist add = type into `input[aria-label="New checklist item"]` then `button:text-is("Add")` (exact — plain :has-text("Add") hits "Add Contingency"); contingencies `input[aria-label="Contingency 1 trigger|outcome"]` + `select[aria-label="Contingency 1 branch"]` (persisted value = slug e.g. `alternate`); Mark as Played lives INSIDE the edit modal and pops a `prompt` "Session recap (optional)" → `session-played` log; quick "Activate" ⇒ change-data `__map__.activeMapName`; quick "To Initiative" on statless NPC throws console Error + inline `.sessions-form-error` (Marta Copperkettle = permanent statless example — needs Stats tab AC/HP before init).
- EB SAVE/LOAD: save modal `.encounter-modal-save input`→Save (silent, verify via GET /encounters savedAt+effectiveXP); load list `.encounter-list > *` rows [Load|pen|trash], trash = instant delete; row scoping by exact `td` text ("Wolf"⊂"Dire Wolf"). Music track editor is INLINE `.music-editor` (`#music-editor-urls`); invalid save now shows inline `.music-editor-error` banner (FIXED 2026-10-24, was console-only). Party inventory add modal `#pi-item-name/#pi-item-qty/#pi-item-desc`; desc autofill LIVE (FIXED 2026-10-24 — reads `description`, fills equipment.json text on exact name match). Music mood select auto-plays AND logs `type:'music'`.
- SSE OBSERVER PROBE (2026-10-24, map-editor fix): sseClient `handlers` is a **Set**, not a Map — "wrap existing handlers in place" via Object.entries(new Set) silently wraps nothing while still reporting size; reliable additive probe = `entry.handlers.add(observer)` (never breaks existing delivery). Manager-open maps: Open seam must emit suffix-free mapName (server publishes `map-data-<c>-<name>` suffix-free, server/routes/maps.js); `.json` suffix in props = expectedKey mismatch, deaf editor (FIXED MapsManager.jsx).
- INLINE-ERROR CONVENTION (2026-10-24): the app's pattern is literally `.{area}-form-error` (`.quests-form-error`/`.npcs-form-error`/…; `.ct-*-error` shorthand does NOT exist) — copy the block, share `--color-error-border`/`--background-color-error`/`--color-error-light` tokens from index.css. Music edit-save writes whole `music` object to change-data instantly via useSyncedState (no 10s debounce wait to verify persistence).
- MOCK-GROUNDED RULE DATA (2026-10-24, party-inventory autofill fix): a `vi.mock` of `dataLoader.loadEquipment` that fabricates rows with the WRONG key (`desc`) PASSES against broken code — mocks for rule-data consumers must use disk-shaped rows (`description` array-of-paragraphs in equipment.json) or read the real file (toolValidation.services.test.js pattern). When fixing a key-mismatch bug, audit sibling tests' mock shapes too.
- SESSIONS QUICK "TO INITIATIVE" GATE (2026-10-24, FIXED): chip disabled via `combatReady:npcHasStatBlock(n)` stamped onto picker options in `loadResourceOptions` (SessionPlannerModal LinkPicker `initBlocked`); handler pre-validates + `.sessions-toast` (success path calls `onViewInitiative()` — view switches, modal unmounts under your click, re-nav Sessions + reopen; close via `.ct-modal-close`, Escape does NOT close). Stale-enabled repro recipe: PUT npc WITH stats → reopen modal (picker snapshot marks ready) → PUT stats-stripped → real `locator.click()` fires handler pre-check deterministically. `hitPoints:"9 (2d8)"` dice-strings fail `npcHasStatBlock` (Number()→NaN) — gates OFF in both views, consistent not a defect.
- DELETE-GUARD AUDIT RESOLVED (2026-10-24): EB saved-encounter trash AND party-inventory item delete DO confirm (`window.confirm`) — EncounterModal.jsx:57 (since b0cab1579) and PartyInventory.jsx:518 (since 6f96e617f); the 2026-10-13 "no confirm" observation was the Playwright auto-accept artifact (§11). Guard ownership test-pinned by `EncounterBuilder.delete-confirmation.test.jsx` (exactly ONE confirm per click, leaf-formatted prompt). Cheap proof pairing: confirm-override record + `browser_network_requests` diff filtered `/api/.*(inventor|encounter)` = zero DELETEs before accept.
- DUP-NAME ERROR CLEAR (2026-10-24, FIXED): Quests+Factions name edits now clear an exact-match duplicate-name banner on change (`handleFormChange` guards `error === DUPLICATE_NAME_ERROR`; unrelated save/delete errors stay). Two UX lanes coexist by design: Factions = derived/while-typing (`getDuplicateState`), Quests/NPCs = click-guard; client message strings are byte-identical to server `utils/nameUniqueness.js` output — anchor exact-string clears on that constant. Harness mocks (`useEntityManagement`/`factionsState`) capture save spies AT RENDER time — mid-test reassignment of `state.saveXList` never reaches the mounted component; steer with `mockRejectedValueOnce` on the original reference.
- MARKDOWN ROW PREVIEWS (2026-10-24, quests fixed via Notes pattern): compact list rows render markdown with shared `src/components/common/MarkdownPreview.jsx` + a CSS block clamping `.markdown-preview` children (2-line `-webkit-line-clamp`, nested-block inline margins) — copy Notes.css, don't hand-roll. `renderMarkdown` emits a trailing `\n` inside the preview div → textContent assertions must trim. Chromium reports `display: flow-root` for `-webkit-box` clamp — clamp still honored, not a defect.
- EB SAVE/LOAD TOASTS (2026-10-24, added): local `showToast` + `.encounter-toast`/`.encounter-toast-error` (role=status, 2.5s display-only auto-hide) in EncounterBuilder — mirrors `.sessions-toast`; save now AWAITS update/save (was fire-and-forget), failures toast instead of silence; internal XP-reconcile write on load stays fire-and-forget (deliberately no toast). Pitfall: header Save button keeps `aria-label:"Save encounter"` in update mode (only `title` flips to "Update encounter") — target by title in tests.
- MAP CELL data-cell AFFORDANCE (2026-10-24, added): every grid cell is now an invisible `rect.grid-cell[data-cell="gx,gy"]` (fill:none, pointer-events default — don't intercept drags) in GridAndWalls.jsx; walls/fog rects carry the same `data-cell` key. Canonical full-grid query = `rect.grid-cell[data-cell]` (count = gridSize²); raw `[data-cell]` exceeds it when walls/fog exist (by design). Paint/fog automation: click center of `rect.grid-cell[data-cell]` boundingClientRect — replaces grid-bg÷cols viewBox math.
- REVEALED PRUNE SEAM (2026-10-24, added): server/routes/maps.js `pruneRevealedToGrid(mapData)` applied at GET + PUT + PUT-description — canonical single home for out-of-bounds `revealed` filtering ("gx,gy" strings, keep 0≤g<gridSize); returns SAME object when clean (no churn, identity-assertable in tests); no client-side prune duplicates. Consequence accepted: shrinking gridSize permanently forgets pruned cells. Dev `npm run api` has NO hot reload — restart the API after route edits. Deaf manager-open editor tabs (key-mismatch bug) keep un-pruned in-memory arrays until reload — harmless (fog math ignores OOB; their PUTs still server-pruned).
- MAP FOLD POLICY (2026-10-24): `docs/app-exploration.md` is a live-claims document — fold it when a cycle completes (backlog empty after e2e-implement): full map → `docs/app-exploration-archive-<YYYY-MM>.md`, fresh stub carries only Priority Targets (promoted untested/blocked items) + Depth Seeds (shallow coverage rows) marked *(carried — unverified)*. Facts must be re-derived live + dated by the verifying run; Playwright auto-accept and un-recorded "no confirm/works" claims never count as re-derived. Durable mechanics move ONE way: map→playbook only, never the reverse. Prior full map: `app-exploration-archive-2026-10.md`.
- TRANSFER ROUTE TRAP (2026-10-24 explore): canonical currency/item transfer endpoint is `POST /api/:c/inventory/transfer` ONLY — bare `POST /api/:c/transfer` is swallowed by the change-data `:key` wildcard and silently stores the payload as runtime key `transfer` returning generic "Data saved successfully" (200, no error). Server transfer is atomic + over-balance-gated 409 `Party has only N gp` (routes/inventory.js:299-339); directions `party-to-player`|`player-to-party`; writes char `inventory.currency`+mirror `inventory.gold` direct to char JSON (bypasses change-data) and logs `inventory_transfer`.
- GIVE MODAL DEFAULTS FULL BALANCE (2026-10-24): `.pi-modal` number inputs prefill the FOCUSED currency with the entire party balance (not 0) — read `.pi-currency-give-row input` value BEFORE any Give click; React-native value-setter injection does NOT reach the payload (probe sent modal default, not injected 999). `button:has-text('Give')` also matches every per-row Give chip — scope inside `.pi-modal`.
- CURRENCY CHIP CLICK PHYSICS (2026-10-24, bug-party-inventory-currency-rapid-click-collapse): same-frame synthetic multi-clicks on `.pi-step-btn` collapse to ONE increment (stale-closure `handleCurrencyAdjust` PartyInventory.jsx:457; each click re-POSTs whole inventory, last response wins). Clicks ≥~300ms apart accumulate correctly. Rapid +200×2 once collapsed to 0 client+server. For scripted +N: click once per tick, verify amount after each.
- PLAYER CLIENTS AUTO-PUT MAPS (2026-10-24, bug-player-clients-clobber-fog-reset): read-only (LAN-URL) tabs running the map view issue `PUT /maps/:map` (exploration-memory merge, Map.jsx:223-233 effect runs on ALL clients) + `POST __map__`; an open player tab re-persists its stale fully-revealed set within ~5s of a GM fog seed/reset, wiping it. Any fog authoring must CLOSE player tabs first (or fix landed) — verify disk `revealed` count stays put before judging fog render.
- LOOT TWO-STAGE + XP LEDGER (2026-10-24): Generate Loot fills `.initiative-loot-textarea` + XP bar; stage-1 `Award Loot` (`.initiative-btn-loot`) only reveals stage-2 confirm (`button.initiative-btn-complete` + Cancel); stage-2 writes `loot` + `encounter loot_awarded` log entries IMMEDIATELY (POST /log, not debounced) and grants runtime `xp` = floor(totalXP/pcCount) to EVERY party PC, then wipes panel. UPDATE 2026-10-24 (FIXED): stage-2 now ALSO credits PURE-CURRENCY loot lines (e.g. "2270 platinum pieces") to party inventory via new atomic `POST /api/:c/inventory/deposit` `{currency:{pp,gp,sp,cp}}` (server re-reads ledger, items untouched, publishes `inventory-${c}` SSE) + writes `inventory_transfer` log `Loot currency deposited to party inventory: N pp…`. Item lines with gp VALUES ("Pearl, 100 gp", "Scale Mail (15 gp)") are NOT cash — parser credits only lines fully consumed by `<num> <unit>` tokens. Stage-2 shows `.initiative-loot-deposit-note` ("+2270 pp to party inventory") when parsed deposit > 0. Deposit failure never blocks the award (console.error, no phantom log). Judge awards by log delta + `/change-data '<PC>'.xp` + GET `/inventory`.
- CT-LIST ROWS HAVE NO BUTTONS (2026-10-24): shared `.ct-list-item` rows carry `aria-label="Edit <Entity>: <name>"` on the LI itself; delete lives INSIDE the opened edit card (in-card `Delete` + native confirm; quest confirms name the object, NPC/settlement/session generic). `page.click('button:has-text("<NPC name>")')` hits the DISABLED `.npcs-init-btn` and 30s-timeouts — open rows via the li aria-label selector. MCP auto-accepts these confirms and `browser_handle_dialog` then errors "no modal state" — verify by list/API state, not the dialog tool.
- EDIT WIZARD + GENERATOR OVERWRITE (2026-10-24): wizard step-1 empty/whitespace name disables `✓ Save` silently (no inline error); destructive edits (level 20→19) save with NO confirm, disk GET-verify is the only gate. `Generate Settlement` replaces ALL current form fields (name/tags/pop/desc) with generator output, no warning — run generators BEFORE typing custom content or copy user values out first.
- MAP FOG MODEL (2026-10-24): fog = cells ∉ (persisted `revealed` ∪ current LoS `visible`); Reset Fog := `revealed := visible` (Map.jsx:237) — NO-OP when LoS already covers grid (11 scattered tokens + a single wall column still saw all 400 on test-map; fog needs real enclosure, and per-cell LoS bresenham from EVERY token is generous). Paint mode single-click = WALL brush (persists `walls:["gx,gy"]`); Erase removes one wall/click. Max-HP spinbutton width:38px visually clips 3-digit values ("178" reads "17…") — read `input.value`, never innerText. `GET /maps/:map` wraps `{map:{...}}` w/ keys walls/revealed/fog/paintCells/players(+gridX/gridY)/zoom/pan.

## 2026-10-24 fix cycle — NEW durable entries
- RAPID-CLICK CHIP RACE (2026-10-24, FIXED PartyInventory.jsx): same-frame synthetic multi-clicks on +/- store chips collapse to one increment when the handler computes from the closure snapshot — repro in vitest needs raw `el.click()` ×N inside ONE `act()` block (sequential `fireEvent.click` flushes per click and CANNOT reproduce); chips gated `disabled={saving}` swallow loop-clicks in tests — poll re-enable between clicks. Canonical fix pattern (mirrors `useMapLoader` pendingSaveRef/flushSave): synchronous `inventoryRef` as accumulation base + one-in-flight-POST queue with trailing coalesce + `localRevRef` revision stamp to drop stale response/echo writes (`setInventory(saved)` from out-of-order responses = full-revert vector). Server writes in arrival order — serialization is client-side; no timers needed (guard:no-timers stays clean).
- CAMPAIGN-LOCK OFF-TARGET NOISE (2026-10-24): loaded tabs auto-POST `/api/campaigns/Frostfall/__campaign__` even under CAMPAIGN_LOCK=test-campaign dev runs — server 403s it and locks, but expect repeated `[campaign-lock] BLOCKED` lines in the dev log during localhost sessions; NOT your writes — do not chase, but do verify your own requests are test-campaign-scoped. Same auto-write lane family as the player-client fog clobber bug (clients persist state they merely hold).
- FOG REPRO NEEDS SEALED POCKETS (2026-10-24): a lone wall column leaves ALL 400 cells in LoS (per-token Bresenham is generous) — `revealed` legitimately recompacts to 400, zero fog. Seal a pocket fully (e.g. column x=10 + 5×5 box x15-19/y15-19 → 20 fogged interior cells) so hidden cells are unreachable from every token.
- VITE PROXY DEFEATS SERVER ISLOCALHOST (2026-10-24): `vite.config.js` proxies /api with `changeOrigin:true` — LAN-tab browsing on :5173 reaches Express :80 with Host rewritten to localhost, so server-side isLocalhost guards NEVER bite vite-proxied player traffic; the CLIENT isLocalhost gate is the operative control there. Server 403 guard only protects direct :80 access (the "Player's: http://<lan>" URL); prove it with explicit `-H 'Host: <non-localhost>'` curl probes. Player-write assertions: capture on the LAN tab index via browser_network_requests({static:false, filter:'/api/campaigns/test-campaign/(maps/<map>|__map__)'}) after the ~5s clobber window.
- READ-ONLY MAP LANES (2026-10-24, FIXED): map authority = localhost/GM. Client gate in Map.jsx revealed-merge/resetFog + useMapLoader isLocalhost param (flushSave/autosave/create all bail; players receive via useSSESync untouched); MapContextSync mirrors `__map__` locally with skipSync=true on read-only clients (grid math works, zero POSTs); server maps.js 403s non-localhost indoor PUT/DELETE/rename/activate/description (OUTDOOR hex PUT stays open — party-travel lane); `__map__` registered in keyValidators.js always-engaged GM authority (denied-* SSE + enforcement_denied log). Fog render marker on player tab: `rect.fog-cell-player`.
- WIZARD LIVE NAME GUARD × SUBMISSION LANE (2026-10-24, FIXED): `CharacterCreationWizard.jsx` name-guard effect now raises `CHARACTER_NAME_REQUIRED_ERROR` live (blank/whitespace name → inline `.error-message` under the input, reusing the submit-validators' string; duplicate still `DUPLICATE_CHARACTER_NAME_ERROR`). Consequence for tests: any `CharacterCreationWizard.submission.test.jsx`-style lane submitting through mocked validators MUST give `mockFormData.name` a non-empty value or the live guard writes an error before submit and the lane's `setErrors`-never-called happy-path pin trips. Partial `vi.mock` of `config/utils.js` in this folder must include BOTH error exports (vitest strict-export errors otherwise). Save path single for both rulesets.
- LEVEL-CHANGE CONFIRM RECIPE (2026-10-24, FIXED): `useCharacterWizard.handleEditWizardComplete` gates a level diff behind native `window.confirm('Change <name>'s level from <A> to <B>? …')` BEFORE the save PUT; Cancel aborts + wizard stays open. Verify by overriding `window.confirm` to capture messages + return false/true across sequential saves, instrument `fetch` to count PUTs, and disk-GET at +12s (changeData debounce). Restore direction produces its own named confirm ("from 19 to 20?") — count the captured array, never dialog-tool state.
- GIVE MODAL DEFAULT-0 + CONFIRM (2026-10-24, FIXED): `.pi-modal` give inputs now prefill `{}` → render 0 (old `Math.min(1, balance)` prefill gone); submit `disabled` until ≥1 positive amount; `applyGive` fires `window.confirm('Give 50 gp to <Player>?')` before `POST /inventory/transfer` (route unchanged — Give rides transfer, loot rides `/inventory/deposit`). Player-to-party retrieval is a separate flow (PlayersPanel "To Party" direct buttons, no currency inputs). Prove confirm-gate honestly: in `page.evaluate` wrap `window.confirm` (record message, return `window.__giveAnswer`) + `window.fetch` (count `/inventory/transfer`); flip answer false→true between legs; `fetches.length 0→1` + ledger GET in same evaluate is the POST-boundary proof (auto-accept eats dialog-tool state). Controlled number-input artifact: typing 50 into value="0" can leave DOM text "050" pre-blur — judge payload, not input text. Baseline rig: +10 chip ×5 at ≥600ms spacing.
- MUSIC PATCH SHAPE (2026-10-24, FIXED): track editor Save/Reset now POST `/api/campaigns/:c/music` `{overrides}` ONLY (new `saveMusicOverrides` in `src/services/ui/musicService.js`); dedicated server route (campaigns-changedata.js, above the wildcard) intent-split merges `{...stored, ...incoming}` — transport fields (playing/mood/volume) survive, editor save-while-stopped can no longer flip `playing:true`. Transport writes (play/pause via useSyncedState) still POST full `{value:{...}}` = full overwrite. Response echoes `{music}`; editor pins local store skipSync. Toast convention: NO global toast component — per-area `.{area}-toast` (`.sessions-toast`/`.encounter-toast` useState+2500ms auto-hide role=status); `.music-toast` mirrors it; guard auto-flags via /setToast|toast/i. PITFALLS: pre-fix, partial POST `{overrides:{}}` through the generic `/:key` wildcard REPLACED the whole music doc (normalize with full `{value:{mood,playing,volume,overrides}}` if probing); `vi.unstubAllGlobals()` in afterEach removes setup.js's EventSource stub → later mounts in same file throw `EventSource is not defined` — use `vi.spyOn(globalThis,'fetch')` + mockRestore.
- MUSIC OEMBED TITLE ENRICHMENT (2026-10-24, FIXED): POST `/music` route enriches `overrides.<mood>=[{videoId,title}]` server-side via `server/utils/resolveTrackTitles.js` (`resolveOverrideTitles(incoming, base)` before intent-split merge) — bounded `AbortSignal.timeout(5000)` fetch/video, parallel + dedupe, stored-real-title index skips refetch (transport writes echo resolved overrides, play/pause stays instant), any failure keeps raw ID + `console.error`, save NEVER blocks. Track-title's only visible surface = log page "GM set ambience: <Mood> — <title>" (LogMusicEntry). Editor lane recipe: inline editor state (mood, open/closed) persists across Save until Cancel/nav; `browser_type` on `.music-editor-urls` textarea lands cleanly (number-input gotcha §61 does NOT extend here). PLAYWRIGHT MCP arg pitfall: browser_click/browser_select_option need `target` (snapshot ref `fXXeNN`), browser_network_requests needs `static` — `ref` alone rejected "target: Missing key". Code Mode has no setTimeout — sequence bounded awaited tool calls; mid-call ReferenceError still leaves earlier clicks applied (verify state before re-clicking).
- INITIATIVE HP INPUTS SIZE-TO-CONTENT (2026-10-24, FIXED): `.initiative .hp-inline-input` = `width:auto` + `min-width:36px` + `field-sizing:content` in Initiative.css; the old `.hp-max-input{width:30px}` override (the real clipper — backlog's 38px was stale) deleted; both HP siblings symmetric. Max-HP input renders ONLY on monster (npc) cards — player cards show `.hp-max-val` span, premise checks need an EB-joined monster. Gotchas: active-card `.monster-autocomplete-list` can drape over the HP row and survives campaign re-select — `document.activeElement.blur()` dismisses (screenshot-only, metrics unaffected); `.creature-card` has a PRE-EXISTING ~8px horizontal bleed (scrollWidth 218 vs clientWidth 210) from `.creature-name`/autocomplete, never attribute initiative-card overflow to HP inputs without A/B. jsdom has no layout engine (vitest css:false) — computed-width seams untestable, prove via live scrollWidth≤clientWidth.
- CURRENCY CHIP BURST COALESCING (2026-10-24, FIXED): chip clicks now ride `persistCurrency` → trailing debounce 400ms (`CURRENCY_PERSIST_DEBOUNCE_MS`, PartyInventory.jsx:~24; timer :502, guard category network/save debounce) — one whole-store POST per burst, optimistic display instant, queue invariants + rev-stamp from §rapid-click fix intact; items/save/delete stay immediate. `flushCurrencyPersist()` (cancel+POST now) guards atomic `/transfer` legs (Give + confirm-move) and unmount. Live proof recipe: `browser_run_code_unsafe` needs `async (page)=>{}` wrapper; evaluate round-trip ~150-200ms/call so waitForTimeout(120) ≈ 300ms real spacing (inside 400ms window → bursts merge); per-click dispatchEvent('click',{bubbles:true}) accumulates all deltas in one flushed POST. App reassigns window.fetch after mount — re-install spies or use curl for reads. `.pi-currency-row[0]` = Platinum; scope amounts by row label.
- EB GEN MODAL BUDGET + LAST-ROLL (2026-10-24, FIXED): suggestions rows now carry budget indicator vs live Target XP (`targetXP={totalThreshold}` prop-threaded, never re-derived; "120% of target 60,300 · +12,100 over"); over rows `gen-suggestion-over`/`gen-budget-over` (--color-error), within `gen-budget-within`; re-roll collapses prior set into `Previous roll (N suggestions)` toggle (aria-expanded, Apply functional from it, one-deep stack); fresh open clears (component-local state, modal unmounts on close). Difficulty select via native-setter+change while modal OPEN live-updates targetXP — rows re-flag over/within without regenerating (best over-budget demo seam). `Clear All` in EB summary panel = reliable table-selection cleanup. Suggestions never POST to server (modal-local).
- CT DELETE CONFIRMS NAME THE OBJECT (2026-10-24, FIXED): NPCs `Delete NPC "<name>"?`, Sessions `Delete Session Plan "<name>"?`, Settlements `Delete Settlement "<name>"?` — each page owns its handleDelete (no shared factory), message interpolates the `editing*.name` the delete call itself uses; Quests was already canonical (`Delete quest "<name>"?`, Quests.jsx:216). Recipe: one `window.confirm` override plant (`__confirmLog` + `__confirmAnswer`) survives SPA-route changes NPCs→Sessions→Settlements→Admin — arm/disarm per cell, judge Cancel legs by list/disk API state (auto-accept eats dialog-tool state). Delete-accept auto-closes the edit card → stale refs ("Ref not found") — re-snapshot after each delete. `browser_console_messages({all:true})` header counts CURRENT page load only — session-clean verdict is all:false; cross-nav SSE-reconnect + ad-CORS lines are §70 noise.

## 2026-10-24 explore run — FOG OF WAR / MAPS lane (durable rig + clobber-fix re-verification)
- FOG MODEL: `useFogOfWar(players, walls, placedItems, gridSize, revealed)` — vision sources are **`mapData.players` ONLY** (char tokens); NPCs/decor are NOT light sources. fog = every cell not in `revealed` AND not in current LoS (`lineOfSight.js` shadowcast). Persistence is GROW-ONLY (`mergeNewlyRevealed`) and **localhost-gated** (Map.jsx:234 `if (!isLocalhost) return`) — the read-only-client-clobber fix (§player-clients). Players never author map state; they mirror via SSE (`useSSESync`) only.
- RECIPE — seal a fog dungeon: Maps → Generate Dungeon (BSP) → name+grid 30 → disk fresh = walls set + `revealed:[]` (fully fogged). Open it, then in the map's `Items` panel drag a **Character** tile onto a floor cell. **HTML5 drop needs a real `DragEvent`**: Playwright `mouse.down/move/up` alone does NOT trigger the drop (`rect.grid-bg` intercepts pointer-up) — dispatch `new DragEvent('drop',{dataTransfer: DataTransfer.setData('text/plain','character:<Name>'), clientX/clientY at cell center})`. NPC drop payload is `'npc'`. Once the char token lands, its room lights (LoS) and autosaves `revealed` (~10s debounce).
- RECIPE — incremental reveal + doors: **Select-mode token drag DOES work with plain `mouse.down/move/up`** (usePlayerDragging pointer path, distinct from the drop path above) — drag the token through an open doorway and `revealed` grows incrementally+persistently (observed 51→56→62→111→223 through a col-9 passage). First drag can silently NO-OP (token stays put, absorbed-first-action family §126) — retry the identical mouse sequence, then verify `players[0].gridX/gridY` before trusting. Doors default `!item.open` = CLOSED → both LoS blockers AND movement obstacles (reachability.js). Toggle: **right-click a placed door → SVG `g.item-context-menu text.menu-option` "Open Door"** (label flips "Close Door" when open; dismiss `text.menu-close`✕); persists `door.open:true` to disk. Cells at a closed-door THRESHOLD still reveal; cells beyond stay fogged until opened.
- RECIPE — Reset Fog: GM-only (Map.jsx:243). Collapses `revealed := current LoS` (223→156 observed) and is the direct repro for the historical clobber bug. VERIFIED FIXED 2026-10-24: reset HELD past the 6s autosave window and an open LAN player tab did NOT resurrect it (player fog grew 677→744 = revealed 156, matching GM, live via SSE). Player tab authored ZERO `PUT /maps/*` / `POST /__map__` across the whole session (only benign `POST /api/:c/__campaign__ {campaignName}` presence stamps). Closed-map = the correct test surface; the old open test-map (no walls) made Reset Fog look like a no-op.
- SELECTORS: cells `rect.grid-cell[data-cell="gx,gy"]`; fog rects `[class*=fog]` (count = gridSize²−revealed); placed items `g.placed-item` > `rect.item-hit-area` (NO data-cell on items — locate by `document.elementFromPoint(cellCenter)` or svg CTM math, CELL=40px, `p.x=gx*40+20`); Generate modal `.modal-content`; Maps delete = custom modal `.maps-manager-modal-overlay` with buttons "Cancel"/**"Yes, Delete Permanently"** (NOT native confirm — handle by clicking the confirm text, `browser_handle_dialog` won't see it). Active map row shows "Active" badge and its Activate button DISAPPEARS (Open/Rename/Edit description/Delete remain).
- CAMPAIGN LOCK + player Add-campaign (2026-10-24): LAN player view strips GM nav (Encounters/Factions/NPCs/Quests/Sessions/Settlements/Admin/Music gone; Maps→"Map") but STILL exposes the enabled **"+ Add" → "Create New Campaign"** modal (`CampaignSelection.jsx` has no isLocalhost awareness). Under `dev:locked` the Create POSTs `/api/campaigns` → **403 "Campaign locked to test-campaign"** and the component's `if(error)` top-level screen renders that message + a Reload button (feedback IS shown — NOT silent). Server-side lock is the real gate; folder list verified unchanged after a player create-attempt. UPDATE (b0337f5d5 + re-verified live 2026-10-24 pm): Add/Create are now HIDDEN on non-localhost — campaign picker is list-only for players.
- PLAYER-VIEW GATE ASYMMETRY (2026-10-24 pm, LIVE BUG): campaign-add hidden + maps lane zero-write, BUT the initiative lane is NOT gated — `Next →` from LAN origin POSTs `/combatSummary`+`/activeCreatureName`+`/__initiative__`+`/lastAppliedTurnStartCreature` (all 200) and ADVANCES the shared turn; Clear/+NPC render enabled too (bug-player-clients-can-hijack-initiative). The read-only-vs-writable asymmetry is the fingerprint.

## 2026-10-24 pm explore run — CRUD/Campaign-management lane (durable)
- NPC STATS-GATE TRAP (LIVE BUG): `npcHasStatBlock` (npcStatBlockUtils.js:13-15) does `Number.isFinite(Number(npc.hitPoints))` — `"9 (2d8)"` → NaN → gate FALSE. Marta Copperkettle ships disk-canonical `hitPoints:"9 (2d8)"` with AC 11 and is PERMANENTLY blocked from every initiative lane (NPC row chip, Sessions `To Initiative`, `Initiative.jsx:263` +NPC filter) with a FALSE "Fill in AC and HP" tooltip. Differential proof: rewrite HP to `"9"` → chips open; restore `"9 (2d8)"` → re-seals. Until fixed, harnesses needing an NPC in initiative must normalize HP to numeric first (and restore after).
- PARTY INVENTORY API SHAPES: GET/PUT `/api/:c/inventory` wraps `{inventory:{currency:{pp,gp,sp,cp},items:[{id,name,quantity,description}]}}` — keys pp/gp/sp/cp, NOT "gold"/"silver" (DOM labels are Platinum/Gold/…). Currency minus-sign chip text is U+2212 ("−1"), not ASCII hyphen — match accordingly. Same-tick DOM reads after chip bursts can show PRE-balance (React flush lag): judge by disabled-chip pattern or ≥400ms-delayed GET.
- ITEM MOVE LANE: row `Move` → modal "Move "<item>" to Player" (player combobox, qty spinner capped at party count with decrease disabled, preview copy). No native confirm — executes silently, atomic both legs (`To Party` on From Players row reverses; char JSON `inventory` + party `items` flip in one shot). Item qty inline stepper clamps at 1 (row never auto-deletes at 0; delete via `Delete item` + confirm).
- FACTIONS/NOTES CRUD: faction dup-name guard is LIVE-typing (`.factions-form-error`, Save disabled, clears when name made unique — Factions.jsx). Influence = range slider min 1 max 10 (NOT a number input). Notes have NO name field — title = partyLocation; row aria-label = RAW markdown description (anchor `[aria-label^="Edit note: "]` + description prefix). Party Level auto-derives (party avg 19 observed). Delete confirms on Factions/Notes remain generic ("Delete this faction?"/"Delete this note?").
- SESSIONS PLANNER: link comboboxes are add-on-select (`+ Link X…` resets after add; proof-of-link = server `links.*` + auto-generated checklist items `auto-enc-<slug>`/`auto-npc-<slug>` appended + progress bar re-counts; do NOT trust the select's displayed value). Auto-checklist items are GM-toggleable checkboxes. `Suggest XP Budget`/`Generate Rumors` buttons present unpressed (AI lane).
- EB SAVE/LOAD: `Save encounter` opens a NAME modal (input+Save) → toast "Encounter saved" + `POST /encounters` 201. `Load encounter` modal rows carry aria `Load|Rename <name>|Delete <name>` buttons; **in-modal Delete succeeds but the modal stays open** (row removed; close × yourself). Gen modal fresh-open empty-state is BY DESIGN (in-session previous-roll only). Sidebar `Encounters`/`Initiative` nav from other views works; URL-bar nav does not (§95 holds).
- INITIATIVE CLEAR + STRIP: `Clear` → auto-handled confirm → joined monsters RENAME to "NPC N", PCs stay (12), round reseeds 1 (§33 holds). Removing "NPC 1": text-scan /NPC 1/ matches all 12 target-selects — anchor `input.value==='NPC 1'` → `.closest('.creature-card')` → `.npc-remove-btn` (confirm auto-accepts instantly; §113 pattern). NPC cards have NO `img[alt="NPC 1"]` — name lives in an input.
- SIDEBAR ROLLERS: dice-tray `d20` click → `POST /log` `roll/dice "Dice Tray d20" characterName:"GM"` — cheapest cross-tab SSE log carrier (no combat needed). **The dice-tray result popup NEVER auto-dismisses and intercepts all page clicks until its × is clicked** — always sweep `document.querySelector('.dice-tray-popup-overlay')` before batch clicking elsewhere. Ability SCORE cells clicked while no combat ran still logged 4 `roll` entries with NO visible popup (judge by log delta).
- CAMPAIGN ADMIN SNAPSHOT APIs: `POST /api/:c/admin/snapshot` (200, silent — no toast), `GET /api/:c/admin/snapshots` → `{snapshots:[{filename,size,timestamp}]}` (newest-first; keep-10), `GET /api/:c/admin/download` → application/zip. **Bare `GET /api/:c/snapshots` (no /admin) is swallowed by the change-data wildcard → `{value:null}`** — same trap family as bare `/transfer`. Snapshots survive post-run cleanup; naming a leftover snapshot in reports matters because Rollback targets the newest.
- EB AUTOCOMPLETE GHOST: `.monster-autocomplete-list` opened by EB search survives SPA nav to Initiative (rect may sit offscreen `x:-66` or drape over card inputs and eat clicks); a TRUSTED click on a non-interactive target (e.g. the "Initiative (round 1)" heading ref) dismisses; `document.body.click()` synthetic does NOT. Bug filed (bug-eb-autocomplete-ghost-blocks-initiative-clicks).
- WIZARD CONFIRM VERBATIM: `"Change <Name>'s level from 19 to 20? This updates the character in the live campaign."` — capture with modal-state echo before handle_dialog accept; the dialog-tool modal state shows message text reliably here (unlike some ct-modal confirms).
- MUSIC STATE = CHANGE-DATA KEY: music rides `characterChangeData.music` (campaigns-changedata.js:96-121, intent-split merge + `change-<c>-music` SSE publish) — **Admin "Clear Change Data" wipes overrides AND mood/playing** → GET `/api/:c/music` then returns the wildcard shape `{value:null}` (music key absent) = fully-clean verdict, NOT a route failure. Players cast with defaults until next save.

## 2026-10-24 fix-run — NPC initiative lane (durable, from bug-npc-dice-formatted-hp fix)
- NPC WEAPON-CONFIRM POLARITY TRAP: Playwright auto-accepts `window.confirm`; in `addNPCToInitiative` accept=TRUE means "go provide a weapon" → `'needs-weapon'` refusal (opens editor). To land a live join for an attackless NPC: stub `window.confirm = () => false` in evaluate, then synthetic-click the chip.

- NPC row add-to-initiative chip absorbs first trusted clicks (row-open wins) — fresh `boundingBox` + `mouse.click`, or second ref click, lands it.

## 2026-10-24 improve run — NEW durable entries
- Enforcement master-off now writes blob-wide zero `{...ENFORCEMENT_DEFAULTS, mode:'off'}` incl. unsurfaced serverDice (161ee52c1) — OFF→ON round trip inherently never re-arms sub-flags; harnesses can trust mode:'off' ⇒ every sub-flag false on disk.

- Enforcement baseline in test-campaign is key-absent (`GET /change-data` → `{"value":null}`), not `{mode:'off'}` — restoring "as found" after a rig requires Admin clear-change-data (no per-key DELETE reverts a top-level special key to absent).

- PITFALL enforcement hint copy: EnforcementSection.test guards `queryByText(/Referee/i)` (removed assignable-role regression) — any new hint text naming "referee" breaks that guard; say "AI dice rulings" (the schemas.js 'dice' ruling term) instead.
